# Hungarian–English consolidated dictionary

Scope: source-bound Hungarian study edition. K IDs have explicit 2025 dates. U IDs have no known year and are a separate sequence, not assumed to follow 2025. Catalogue IDs are generated, not original lesson numbers. Source quotations preserve original errors and answers. Canonical grammar summaries are editorial consolidations of explicit teaching material. OCR is provisional, especially for Hungarian diacritics and table alignment. Eight MP3 files and speech in three MP4 files remain NOT TRANSCRIBED.

# Hungarian–English dictionary

1263 consolidated headwords and expressions. Case-insensitive NFC deduplication merges repeated workbook sheets and selected inflected/typo forms. Multiple meanings remain together. This is a source-bound inventory, not a claim of exhaustive coverage of untranscribed speech or unproofread image fine print.

First encounter is the earliest matched evidence **within each separate sequence**, not a proven date of first learning. Subsequent lists mean recurrence; an expansion must be established from the cited source. Forms are attested spellings, sometimes from learner exercises; canonical forms are in the linked grammar reference. Example excerpts retain source wording and are not a corrected answer key.

Grammar reference · Review issues · Main index
